One test: its file, its input, its fragment context (empty for a
document), its scripting (`on`, `off`, or `both`), and the tree
expected, in the files' dump format without the `| ` prefixes.

    export class TreeTest(
      public file: String,
      public input: String,
      public fragment: String,
      public scripting: String,
      public tree: String,
    ) {}

thousands of calls each backend would compile.

    export let treeTests(): List<TreeTest> {
      let all = new ListBuilder<TreeTest>();
      let rows = treeTestsText().split("\u{E001}");
      for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i += 1) {
        let f = rows[i].split("\u{E002}");
        all.add(new TreeTest(f[0], f[1], f[2], f[3], f[4]));
      }
      all.toList()
    }
